# MEMORY_BANK Archive — Historical Current State Snapshots

**Purpose:** Full-text archive of superseded `## Current State Snapshot` entries from `11_MEMORY_HANDOFF/MEMORY_BANK.md`. `MEMORY_BANK.md` keeps exactly one authoritative current-state block; every prior snapshot is moved here verbatim (not summarized, not edited) when it is superseded, so no history is lost and the live file stays uncluttered.

**Created:** 2026-07-11 (T-334/E1, Workstream E meta-process hygiene) — consolidating the snapshot chain that had grown to 11 entries in `MEMORY_BANK.md`, including two same-day (2026-07-08) triples and, as of this pass, a live pair of unsuperseded same-day (2026-07-11) entries that were the proximate trigger for this consolidation (recurrence of the GG-082 narrative-conflation pattern). See `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-11 (T-334 entry) for the consolidation rationale, and `scripts/check_memory_bank_snapshots.sh` for the automated guard that now warns if this is allowed to recur.

**Ordering:** reverse-chronological (newest archived entry first), matching the convention already used in the live file.

## T-370 — Merge PR #26 (`session/20260715T145241Z-gg-oddburn-t368`, odd-GG-number burn-down) into `main`, reconciled with the parallel even-numbered parity session's branch — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-15

**Done via a dedicated reconciliation session, isolated worktree `session/20260715T-ggreconcile-merge` (Hard Rule 10).** Both PR #25 (even) and PR #26 (odd) merged cleanly at the individual gap-row level as designed — zero collisions in `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` gap bodies. `DECISION_LOG.md` had one pure-append collision (HEAD's T-368/T-369 close-out block vs. even branch's 17 rows), resolved by concatenating both in full. `.last_session_checkpoint`/`.gitattributes` conflicted (PR #26 introduced GG-103's append-only reformat) and were resolved preserving all three data points across the format transition.

**Substantive finding the reconciliation caught that neither isolated branch could have found alone:** GG-103's fix (PR #26) reformatted `.last_session_checkpoint` to append-only, but `scripts/write_session_checkpoint.sh` and `.claude/hooks/session_guard.sh` — both living on `main` via the separately-merged T-368/T-369 work that predates both branches' fork points — still assumed the old single-overwrite format. Left unfixed, the next session-end would have silently destroyed the new append-only log, and every future session-start would have permanently misreported a corrupt checkpoint pointer. Fixed both scripts + rewrote `tests/test_write_session_checkpoint.py` for append semantics; corrected GG-103's own Resolution text (previously understated as 3 files touched, actually 5). A second pass also caught that the test-file rewrite had been verified-passing but never actually staged/committed in the first reconciliation commit — fixed in a follow-up commit (`7cdb4ee`) before merging, so this "committed but not really" loose end didn't survive into `main`.

**Merged via PR #27** (https://github.com/eignacio1/prediction-market-bot/pull/27), CI green, then `gh pr merge 27 --merge`. PR #25 and #26 both auto-flipped to MERGED status once their commits landed on `main` via the `--no-ff` merges — no separate close needed. All three worktrees (reconciliation, odd, even) and their local + remote branches retired post-merge.

**OPEN gap count, `governance_scan.sh`'s own `grep "| OPEN"` methodology, applied identically pre/post:** 15→10 (119→120 total gaps). Full suite: 2718 passed / 29 skipped / 0 failed, confirmed twice (pre-merge and via GitHub Actions CI on PR #27).

**Caveat surfaced, not fixed:** that same grep only matches literal `"| OPEN"` and misses `Status` cells that bold-prefix the word (`**OPEN`) — undercounts the true OPEN backlog. Not registered as its own GG this pass to avoid scope creep on a reconciliation session; flag for a future session if picked up. See `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 (reconciliation-merge row) for full detail.

## T-371 — GG-119/ADR-0045: fix T1 MLB NO-direction vig sign flip in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py::_directional_edge()` — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-15/16

**Isolated worktree `session/20260715T200655Z-gg119-t1-vig-sign-fix`.** Mirrored GG-113/ADR-0043's T4M-PROJ fix pattern onto T1's own gating file: the NO branch was negating the whole half_spread-inclusive `gross_edge`, converting `half_spread` from a cost into a credit for all 248 NO-direction resolved bets pre-fix (74 of which only cleared `MIN_EDGE_TO_APPROVE` due to the inflation). Implemented Proposed D1 exactly: NO branch changed from `-gross_edge` to `-gross_edge - 2.0*half_spread`; `paper_trader.py::open_position()`'s band gate extended from `{"T4M-PROJ"}` to `{"T4M-PROJ","T1"}`. Both the write-path fix and band-gate extension landed together in one commit (`53a9208`) — unlike GG-113, no self-correction/second commit was needed.

`CURRENT_LOGIC_EPOCH` advanced 4→5 (later 5→6 via T-373/GG-123, T2-scoped, see that entry — non-overlapping boundary). Regression tests added/split across `test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py::TestDirectionalEdge`, `test_approval_path_invariants.py::TestFeeSignInvariance`, `test_paper_trader.py::TestOpenPosition`, `test_backfill_logic_epoch.py`. Full suite 2719/1-fail/29-skip → **2720 passed, 0 failed, 29 skipped**.

PR #28 (`6f37629`, fix) + PR #29 (`f557446`, GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md RESOLVED write-up + DECISION_LOG.md entry) both merged to `main`, VPS auto-deploy confirmed green both times. **Live post-reset T1 ledger pulled from VPS: `raw_n=259, clean_n=0`** — the go-live clock restarts from zero, as expected and explicitly accepted by Earl at sign-off. T2 files never touched (parallel session's scope, GG-123). GG-119 flipped RESOLVED. See ADR-0045 (ACCEPTED), `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-119, `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 (T-371 row).

## T-396 — Window C: GG-124 measurement-layer fix (per-band clean-epoch floors) + C7 monitor verification — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-23/24

**Isolated worktree `session/20260723T233920Z-c-measurement`.** Scope was strictly C2 + C3 of `ITERATION_PLAN_MC_REMEDIATION_2026-07-22.md` Window C.

**C2 — per-band clean-epoch floors (commit `abd92ae`):** `logic_epoch.py`'s `_BOUNDARIES` entries gained a `bands_affected` field (`ALL_BANDS` sentinel or an explicit list), verified from each resolving commit's actual diff rather than trusting prior prose. New `clean_epoch_floor_for_band(band)` computes the highest boundary whose `bands_affected` includes that band; `is_clean_for_band(logic_epoch, band)` wraps the comparison. All 6 call sites of the old single-arg `filter_clean(recs)` updated to `filter_clean(recs, band)`: `run_band_monitor.py`, `run_power_calc.py`, `mc_forward_projection.py`, `run_t329_size_weighted_diag.py`, `run_t332_gamestate_diag.py`, `run_t333_edgeformula_diag.py`. Verified floors: T1=5, T2=6, T3=3, T4M-PROJ=8 — previously every band was pinned to the global 8, meaning T1/T2/T3's `clean_n` was being zeroed by epoch bumps (7, 8) that never touched their write paths. This is retroactive over already-stamped data, so T1/T2/T3 should repopulate `clean_n` from historical epoch≥5/≥6/≥3 bets immediately, not just from new accrual.

**C3 — C7 monitor verification (no new code):** confirmed `run_band_monitor.py::analyze_band()` + `monitor.py::report_band_monitor_verdicts()` (T-341, pre-existing) already implement per-band edge-tercile monotonicity + calibration-slope CI checks, both warn-only (never call `trigger_kill_switch`, per GG-098 discipline) — verified live on VPS cron via `crontab -l` (Mondays 14:20 UTC, last output 2026-07-20). GG-124's blackout, not a missing monitor, was why these alarms weren't firing.

**GG-124 addendum written, gap NOT closed** — the fix repopulates `clean_n` but does not add the warn-at-bump-time mechanism GG-124's own Resolution field calls for; that half remains unowned. Re-check the 2026-07-26 trigger against the new per-band floors.

Tests: 66/66 passing (`test_backfill_logic_epoch.py`, `test_power_calc.py`, `test_band_monitor.py`), fixtures updated to use `clean_epoch_floor_for_band()` instead of hardcoded epoch literals so they don't go stale on the next bump. Full diff vs. `origin/main`: 11 files, 182(+)/45(-).

See `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-23 (T-396 row), `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-124 addendum, `04_EXECUTION/ITERATION_MC_PROGRESS.md`.

## T-387 — Epoch-7 verification contingency FIRED → same-day remediation → epoch 8 registered and live — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-19

**Shared `main` tree, path-scoped commits (`b371e9f`, `eb0bea4`).** Follows T-385 (epoch-7 registration, same session). Full arc:

1. **Verification FAILED as registered:** `pregame_mlb_20260718.json` (15:55 UTC run) carried `ba_l10_shrunk: null` for all 62 players. Root cause: `run_mlb_pregame.py` sources the field from `exp_output/MLB/mlb_feature_matrix.csv`, and the VPS copy was last regenerated by PIPE-05FX (22:30 UTC nightly) 2h **before** the ADR-0047 merge — the column didn't exist. `run_t4_mlb_live.py` fell back to raw `ba_l10` (line 349), so the Jul-18 afternoon slate ran behaviorally epoch-6 while stamping ~200 orders epoch 7. Note the audit-log false negative found along the way: `ba_l10_shrunk` payloads log to `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`, NOT `audit_mlb_t4.jsonl`.
2. **Remediation (operator-selected "fix tonight + epoch 8"):** manual PIPE-05FX re-run (CSV regenerated with column, 18:04 UTC), manual PIPE-06pre re-run (JSON repopulated, exit 0), t4-mlb restart 18:06:52 UTC.
3. **Epoch 8 registered:** boundary = 2026-07-18T18:07:19Z, the first `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl` row with `ba_l10 != ba_l10_shrunk` (empirically verified on the VPS), same resolving commit `f961fba` — an activation-accounting correction, not a logic change. Stamped-7 bets wholly excluded from `clean_n` (stamps govern `filter_clean`; the cohort irreversibly mixes fallback and genuine-shrunk bets; the genuine subset is recoverable only via a DS-authorized re-stamp backfill, deliberately not done). Interim mis-stamped resolutions were accounting-only — no gate/adequacy decision consumed the contaminated clean_n during the token-interrupted gap.
4. **Verified live end-to-end:** 3,004 shrunk-bearing cycle rows / 2,835 distinct through Jul-19; `pregame_mlb_20260719.json` (nightly cron, post-fix CSV) zero nulls — self-heals from Jul-19 onward; VPS at `b371e9f`, t4-mlb restarted 20:41 UTC; post-restart position (20:43 UTC) confirmed stamped `logic_epoch: 8`. Tests 100/100.

See DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-19 (T-387), GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-105 + GG-124 addenda, `11_MEMORY_HANDOFF/CHECKPOINT_20260719_1350.md`.

## T-388 — Bump-time behavioral-activation check for logic-epoch boundaries — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-21

GG-124's process gap had **two** motivating cases (the GG-113/119/123 boundary-stacking blackout; T-387's data-dependency miss). Built the proposed check as `scripts/check_epoch_activation.py` (Python, not the originally-sketched `.sh` — reused the existing `cron_log_aggregator.py` SSH-embedded-script pattern for the remote half):

1. **`check_stacking()`** — warns (never blocks) if more than one `logic_epoch.py` boundary landed within a trailing 7-day window (GG-124's own "one week" re-check horizon, reused as the threshold). Takes injectable `boundaries`/`now` so it's unit-testable without mutating real dated history.
2. **`check_field_activation(field, artifact_glob)`** — SSHes to the VPS, finds the most recently modified file matching the glob, and confirms the named field is present/non-null in at least one record. Zero-present-with-nonzero-total is flagged as the exact GG-105/T-387 failure signature (deploy landed, artifact didn't carry the new field yet). Handles no-files-matched, unreachable host, nonzero remote exit, and malformed output as explicit ALERTs.
3. `logic_epoch.py` gained a public `get_boundaries()` accessor (script reads it rather than reaching into the private `_BOUNDARIES` tuple) and a new docstring section pointing at the check.
4. Exit code semantics: stacking is warn-only and never affects exit code (per the original "warn, not block" requirement); field-activation ALERTs return 1.
5. `tests/test_check_epoch_activation.py` — 17 cases (stacking window edges, field present/entirely-null/partially-null/no-files/nonzero-exit/timeout/malformed-output, CLI exit-code paths) via injected boundaries and mocked `subprocess.run`. Full suite: 2751 passed, 28 skipped, 0 failed.

Not wired into a pre-commit hook or the bump workflow itself — it's a standalone script to run manually (`scripts/check_epoch_activation.py [--field X --artifact-glob Y] [--skip-vps]`) before/after committing a boundary, matching the "checklist/script" framing this task was registered under. Automatic invocation from the bump workflow, if wanted, is a future task.

See DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-21 (T-388 row), `scripts/check_epoch_activation.py`, `src/prediction_market_bot/logic_epoch.py`.

2. ✅ **T-232 (GG-077 follow-up) — magnitude quantification CLOSED 2026-07-05, analysis-only; code fix decision still open.** `c5_execution/paper_trader.py::open_position()` stores `fee_net_edge` always YES-frame-signed, never flipped for NO-direction bets (confirmed: the same function DOES flip sign for the sibling `fee_contaminated` field via `_directional_gross`, three lines below, but not for `fee_net_edge` itself), and CHECK 3 (`risk.py`) gates on this buggy value directly. **Quantified 2026-07-05 against 2 independent real VPS datasets** (56 closed positions in `paper_portfolio_mlb.json`; 63 scored records in `audit_mlb_edge_decisions.jsonl`, which also captures rejections) — both agree exactly: 12 flips, 100% false-approvals, 0 false-rejections, a deterministic `2 x fee_per_contract` overstatement for every NO bet (proof: `abs(stored)=|gross_edge|+fee`, `abs(true)=|gross_edge|-fee`). 12/56 (21.4%) of closed positions were approved with true edge below the 0.04 policy floor ($82.50 stake, +$3.48 P&L vs. book-wide +$94.80/$664.69) — still net-positive, thinner than intended. ## SESSION 110 (CONTINUATION #2) — EXP-ARITH-MLB-01 BACKTEST RUNNER BUILT + DEPLOYED; GG-067 DOC CORRECTIONS COMPLETE (2026-07-02)

**Opening instruction (session start, before any compaction, never repeated):** "Session open - then immediately finish off the remaining T-4 sub-band components (most pressing issue is building the EXP-ARITH-MLB-01 backtest runner to start moving T4M-ARITH off blocked status). Any remaining tasks with the T-4 sub-bands needs to be addressed, pushed, committed, logged and completed in this session. Proceed with full institutional depth."

**GG-067 registered and resolved (doc correction only, no code implication):** two false data-source claims discovered in the T4M-ARITH/T4M-FLOW experiment design docs — (1) both docs claimed a per-cycle raw context log already existed; it did not — only `dry_run=True` persisted anything, and that path is not the live production path. (2) `EXP-FLOW-MLB-01.md` additionally claimed `t4_price_cache.py` was the live MLB Kalshi price source; it is not — that module is Phase-8-BLOCKED NBA-oriented infra, never imported by `run_t4_mlb_live.py`'s live loop, which instead populates `live_prices: dict` directly from `pm.mid_price`/`pm.vig`. Both docs corrected to cite the real source, `logs/t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`, once built (see next).

**Production fix (T-219 continuation):** added unconditional per-cycle raw-context logging to `run_t4_mlb_live.py` — every live evaluation cycle now appends a JSONL record (including a `game_date` field, added mid-session after a schema-gap review) to `logs/t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`. New tests added (`TestProductionCycleLog`, including `_REQUIRED_FIELDS` schema assertion). This is what makes the GG-067 doc corrections true rather than aspirational — the log now actually exists in the live code path.

**Backtest runner built:** `scripts/run_exp_arith_mlb_01.py` (36 tests, 36/36 passing) grid-sweeps T4M-ARITH's `entry_window`/`confidence_gate` constants against `logs/t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`, resolving real outcomes via `fetch_actual_hits` (MLB Stats API) through a dependency-injected resolver (`build_resolution_cache(cycles, resolver)`) so CI makes zero live network calls. Key design insight: because `T4MlbArithEvaluator` reuses the exact same frozen binomial `p_hits_gte_threshold(...)` function with the exact same inputs as the already-live T4M-PROJ evaluation, a cycle-log record's `p_t4` field is mathematically identical to `p_arith` for that record — no separate arithmetic re-derivation needed in the backtest. Applies ADR-0024 commercial validation tiers (Tier 1 n≥150, Tier 2 n≥500) and the T-177/GG-037 edge-monotonicity guard (Fisher's exact test → MONOTONIC/ANTI-PREDICTIVE/INCONCLUSIVE) to whatever data accumulates. Chronological (no-shuffle) splitting per project-wide temporal-integrity policy. Kalshi taker fee (`0.07 × mip × (1-mip)`) applied in P&L.

**Result as of this session's close: BLOCKED, not run — by design, not by omission.** The cycle log had zero records at deploy time because the logging feature only went live with this session's own deploy, and no MLB game was in progress at the 16:34 UTC deploy moment. `T4M-ARITH` correctly remains `status="BLOCKED"` in the ADR-0039 sub-band table — infra/code authorization is not the same thing as data existence, which is not the same thing as capital authorization. The runner is tested and ready; it has nothing to backtest yet. Next real game day populates the log; then `scripts/run_exp_arith_mlb_01.py` produces the actual (likely still-INCONCLUSIVE-at-low-n, but real) result.

**T4M-FLOW deliberately not advanced this session** beyond the GG-067 doc correction, per the explicit session-opening instruction prioritizing ARITH. Its backtest runner does not exist yet and cannot be built as a simple copy of ARITH's — F-FLOW-01 (score_diff/inning reconstruction) is still "NOT YET WIRED" (`T4MlbGameContext.score_diff` defaults to `0.0`, never populated from a live poll), which the FLOW runner needs to define its "sustained blowout" sample. This is real, unstarted work, tracked as item 0b above.

**Deploy judgment call (logged in DECISION_LOG.md as `[SESSION 110 CONT. / DEPLOY]`):** unlike SESSION 109's docstring-only VPS sync, this session's change adds a live unconditional file write to the running process's execution path — required an explicit `systemctl restart t4-mlb` (not just `git pull`) to activate. Restart verified clean (`active (running)`, no errors), no open T4 MLB positions were in flight at restart time. Full suite: 2235 passed, 7 skipped, 1 pre-existing unrelated failure (`test_feature_csv_freshness`, stale local fixture, unchanged from prior sessions). ## SESSION 108 — T-217/GG-063 RESOLVED (2026-07-01)

**Operator instruction (verbatim intent): finish T-217 — thread the real Kalshi response body through `submit_order()`'s error path to diagnose the HTTP 410 failures, then verify with a manual test order; scope T-211 under a new task/ADR. - Root cause isolated: Kalshi hard-deprecated `POST /portfolio/orders` (v1) platform-wide (`deprecated_v1_order_endpoint`). NBA's 73% historical success rate predates Kalshi's v1 enforcement (zero HTTP 410s in NBA's 158-bet history); MLB's 100% failure rate is a timing artifact, not a code-path divergence.
- Fix: `kalshi_client.py submit_order()` migrated to `POST /portfolio/events/orders` (v2) with correct schema (`bid`/`ask` side, fixed-point decimal `count`/`price` strings, `time_in_force`, `self_trade_prevention_type`), plus the originally-scoped error-body threading (`_post()`'s `body`/`detail` now reaches the caller instead of being discarded behind a bare `f"HTTP {r.status_code}"`).
- Verified empirically against DEMO with a real manual test order: error type changed 410→409 (`market_closed`, all 60 open KXMLBHIT markets were next-day at test time) — confirms the v1 deprecation wall is gone; full 200/201 confirmation deferred to the next live cron run against a genuinely active market. Ticker-mismatch hypothesis explicitly disproven (`GET /markets/{ticker}` returned HTTP 200 on both DEMO and LIVE).
- New governance: GG-063 flipped to RESOLVED; **GG-064** registered (OPEN, LOW) for the unconfirmed DEMO `open_time` lag observed during verification — needs one real cron-run observation to close either way, zero capital risk.
- Tests: 52/52 kalshi-specific pass; no regressions introduced (17 pre-existing failures are the parallel session's T-212 fixture work, untouched here).
- Scope discipline: only `kalshi_client.py` touched in code; `data_contracts.py`/`validators.py`/the three T-212 pipeline files left untouched per operator instruction.
- **T-211 not started this session** — remains next priority (see "NEXT SESSION — HIGH PRIORITY" above).
- **Still pending at session pause:** ADR-0008 superseding addendum (governance deliverable owed per "migrate now, ADR after"), git commit of `kalshi_client.py` + this session's governance doc edits (must not touch the parallel session's `data_contracts.py`/`validators.py`/pipeline files/DECISION_LOG entry), VPS deploy.

(3) Implied λ compression: model assumes λ=14.0/16.1/19.6 at thr=10/15/20; actual scoring implies λ=12.8/12.8/13.8 → compression factors 0.911/0.792/0.703. (4) Within-series compression worsens: G2 avg_pts=17.3 → G5 avg_pts=7.7 as game plans adapt. Root cause: opp_adj_lambda uses regular-season defensive ratings; Finals defensive intensity suppresses scoring 9-30% beyond what the model accounts for. (5) This is a feature engineering gap, NOT a model class failure. Poisson architecture is valid; the λ input is wrong. |

| # | Task | Status |

| Bloomberg terminal rewrite | **`scripts/live_terminal.py` — multi-panel curses layout replacing global-scroll single-pad approach** | ✅ COMPLETE 2026-06-03 SESSION 27 — Full rewrite: 8 independent panels (PORTFOLIO, LIVE POSITIONS, MARKET SNAPSHOT, TODAY'S BETS, ALERTS, RECENTLY RESOLVED, SYSTEM STATUS, T4 LOG TAIL), each with its own curses pad and `scroll_y`. 2-column layout (56%/44%). Tab/Shift+Tab cycles panel focus (focused title turns cyan bold + `↕NN%` scroll indicator). Arrow keys / PgUp/PgDn / Home/End scroll within focused panel. Footer shows control hints. Layout adapts to terminal size via `_compute_layout()`. Atomic curses update via `doupdate()` — zero flicker. |
| Curses ordering bug fix | **Fixed blank-panel rendering: stdscr erased content areas; pad writes were invisible** | ✅ COMPLETE 2026-06-03 SESSION 27 — Root cause: `draw_panel()` called `ps.pad.noutrefresh()` inside each panel draw, then `stdscr.noutrefresh()` ran last → stdscr's blank content areas overwrote pad content in curses' virtual screen buffer → `doupdate()` rendered blank panels despite data present. Fix: moved all pad `noutrefresh` calls to a separate `refresh_pad()` pass that runs AFTER `stdscr.noutrefresh()`. stdscr wins on borders; pads win on content. Data confirmed flowing: 1416 T4 positions, $499.95 deployed, 3 T1 bets. |

## T-356 — Add `pmbot-web-dashboard` health probe to CLAUDE.md Step 4 operational health check — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-21
- GG-118 found the dashboard's systemd service can OOM-crash-loop (`Restart=always`/`RestartSec=5`) while `systemctl is-active` reports `active` throughout, since it re-enters the active state within ~5s of each restart. Step 4 had no check for this service at all.
- **Done:** added a probe to CLAUDE.md Step 4 — `systemctl show pmbot-web-dashboard.service -p NRestarts,ActiveState,SubState` plus a 24h `journalctl ... | grep -c oom-kill` count. Verified both commands live against the VPS before writing them in (current state: `NRestarts=0`, `ActiveState=active`, 0 oom-kills in the last 24h — healthy). Documented explicitly that `NRestarts`/oom-kill count, not `ActiveState`/`SubState`, is the real signal, since a mid-loop snapshot can show `active`/`running` regardless.
- See CLAUDE.md Step 4 (T-356/GG-118 subsection), GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-118, DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-21 (T-356 row).

- `git branch -a` (session-end Step 8 exit check, GG-113/Window 1 close-out) found two local branches never pushed to `origin` and not ancestors of `main`: `session/20260714T050514Z-gg113-vig-sign-fix` (622 commits behind current `main`, worktree at `../PM-worktrees/20260714T050514Z-gg113-vig-sign-fix`) and `gg113-fix-only-backup`. Fix: confirm worktree is clean (`git status --porcelain` empty) via `scripts/end_session_worktree.sh` or manual check, then `git worktree remove` + `git branch -d` both branches.

No destructive action taken this pass either, same rationale as above — bundle all four branches into one deletion pass next time this task is picked up.
- **`gg113-rebase-clean`, `gg113-fix-only-backup`, and `session/20260715T003811Z-gg105-interaction-model` already removed** by a separate 2026-07-15 session (`session/20260715T235138Z-digest-adoption-timestamp-check`, T-372) — see `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 row for that session's own verification of each. That pass did not touch `session/20260714T050514Z-gg113-vig-sign-fix` (not in its removal list; confirmed still present in `git branch -a` at the start of this session).
- **Closed out 2026-07-15 (this session):** independently re-verified `session/20260714T050514Z-gg113-vig-sign-fix` before deleting rather than trusting the prior entries — `git diff main..branch` showed 7,577 lines total but all substantive hunks were "-" (main-only, i.e. main's forward progress the stale branch lacks); the 134 "+" (branch-only) lines were all stale documentation prose already superseded by newer wording on `main` (checked for secrets/credentials specifically, given the branch's history touches GG-116's leaked-API-key incident — found none, only narrative references to the already-completed rotation). `git cherry main branch` flagged 5 commits as patch-unmatched; inspected each — all are old (May–July) handoff/security commits already reachable through `main`'s actual merge history, not unique work (`git log main..branch --oneline` vs `git log branch..main --oneline` confirms `main` is strictly ahead, branch has zero unique commits). `git show 3abab4e` vs `git show fb13166` confirmed byte-identical patch content (SHA differs only due to cherry-pick). No worktree registered (`git worktree list`) or present on disk at `../PM-worktrees/20260714T050514Z-gg113-vig-sign-fix` — already gone. Deleted the branch locally (`git branch -D`); confirmed absent on `origin` (`git ls-remote --heads origin`, no match) so no remote delete needed. Post-cleanup: `git branch -a` shows only `main` + the unrelated, currently-locked `session/20260715T055856Z-efficiency-recs-fivepack` worktree (a different live session, untouched per Hard Rule 10/GG-101); `main` == `origin/main`; working tree clean. **T-360 fully closed — all four originally-identified stale branches now removed across the two sessions.**

## T-370 — Merge PR #26 (`session/20260715T145241Z-gg-oddburn-t368`, odd-GG-number burn-down) into `main`, reconciled with the parallel even-numbered parity session's branch — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-15

**Done via a dedicated reconciliation session, isolated worktree `session/20260715T-ggreconcile-merge` (Hard Rule 10).** Both PR #25 (even) and PR #26 (odd) merged cleanly at the individual gap-row level as designed — zero collisions in `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` gap bodies. `DECISION_LOG.md` had one pure-append collision (HEAD's T-368/T-369 close-out block vs. even branch's 17 rows), resolved by concatenating both in full. `.last_session_checkpoint`/`.gitattributes` conflicted (PR #26 introduced GG-103's append-only reformat) and were resolved preserving all three data points across the format transition.

**Substantive finding the reconciliation caught that neither isolated branch could have found alone:** GG-103's fix (PR #26) reformatted `.last_session_checkpoint` to append-only, but `scripts/write_session_checkpoint.sh` and `.claude/hooks/session_guard.sh` — both living on `main` via the separately-merged T-368/T-369 work that predates both branches' fork points — still assumed the old single-overwrite format. Left unfixed, the next session-end would have silently destroyed the new append-only log, and every future session-start would have permanently misreported a corrupt checkpoint pointer. Fixed both scripts + rewrote `tests/test_write_session_checkpoint.py` for append semantics; corrected GG-103's own Resolution text (previously understated as 3 files touched, actually 5). A second pass also caught that the test-file rewrite had been verified-passing but never actually staged/committed in the first reconciliation commit — fixed in a follow-up commit (`7cdb4ee`) before merging, so this "committed but not really" loose end didn't survive into `main`.

**Merged via PR #27** (https://github.com/eignacio1/prediction-market-bot/pull/27), CI green, then `gh pr merge 27 --merge`. PR #25 and #26 both auto-flipped to MERGED status once their commits landed on `main` via the `--no-ff` merges — no separate close needed. All three worktrees (reconciliation, odd, even) and their local + remote branches retired post-merge.

**OPEN gap count, `governance_scan.sh`'s own `grep "| OPEN"` methodology, applied identically pre/post:** 15→10 (119→120 total gaps). Full suite: 2718 passed / 29 skipped / 0 failed, confirmed twice (pre-merge and via GitHub Actions CI on PR #27).

**Caveat surfaced, not fixed:** that same grep only matches literal `"| OPEN"` and misses `Status` cells that bold-prefix the word (`**OPEN`) — undercounts the true OPEN backlog. Not registered as its own GG this pass to avoid scope creep on a reconciliation session; flag for a future session if picked up. See `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 (reconciliation-merge row) for full detail.

## T-387 — Epoch-7 verification contingency FIRED → same-day remediation → epoch 8 registered and live — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-19

**Shared `main` tree, path-scoped commits (`b371e9f`, `eb0bea4`).** Follows T-385 (epoch-7 registration, same session). Full arc:

Note the audit-log false negative found along the way: `ba_l10_shrunk` payloads log to `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`, NOT `audit_mlb_t4.jsonl`.
2. **Remediation (operator-selected "fix tonight + epoch 8"):** manual PIPE-05FX re-run (CSV regenerated with column, 18:04 UTC), manual PIPE-06pre re-run (JSON repopulated, exit 0), t4-mlb restart 18:06:52 UTC.
3. **Epoch 8 registered:** boundary = 2026-07-18T18:07:19Z, the first `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl` row with `ba_l10 != ba_l10_shrunk` (empirically verified on the VPS), same resolving commit `f961fba` — an activation-accounting correction, not a logic change. Interim mis-stamped resolutions were accounting-only — no gate/adequacy decision consumed the contaminated clean_n during the token-interrupted gap.
4. **Verified live end-to-end:** 3,004 shrunk-bearing cycle rows / 2,835 distinct through Jul-19; `pregame_mlb_20260719.json` (nightly cron, post-fix CSV) zero nulls — self-heals from Jul-19 onward; VPS at `b371e9f`, t4-mlb restarted 20:41 UTC; post-restart position (20:43 UTC) confirmed stamped `logic_epoch: 8`. ## T-388 — Bump-time behavioral-activation check for logic-epoch boundaries — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-21

GG-124's process gap had **two** motivating cases (the GG-113/119/123 boundary-stacking blackout; T-387's data-dependency miss). Built the proposed check as `scripts/check_epoch_activation.py` (Python, not the originally-sketched `.sh` — reused the existing `cron_log_aggregator.py` SSH-embedded-script pattern for the remote half):

1. **`check_stacking()`** — warns (never blocks) if more than one `logic_epoch.py` boundary landed within a trailing 7-day window (GG-124's own "one week" re-check horizon, reused as the threshold). Takes injectable `boundaries`/`now` so it's unit-testable without mutating real dated history.
2. **`check_field_activation(field, artifact_glob)`** — SSHes to the VPS, finds the most recently modified file matching the glob, and confirms the named field is present/non-null in at least one record. Zero-present-with-nonzero-total is flagged as the exact GG-105/T-387 failure signature (deploy landed, artifact didn't carry the new field yet). Handles no-files-matched, unreachable host, nonzero remote exit, and malformed output as explicit ALERTs.
3. `logic_epoch.py` gained a public `get_boundaries()` accessor (script reads it rather than reaching into the private `_BOUNDARIES` tuple) and a new docstring section pointing at the check.
4. Exit code semantics: stacking is warn-only and never affects exit code (per the original "warn, not block" requirement); field-activation ALERTs return 1.
5. `tests/test_check_epoch_activation.py` — 17 cases (stacking window edges, field present/entirely-null/partially-null/no-files/nonzero-exit/timeout/malformed-output, CLI exit-code paths) via injected boundaries and mocked `subprocess.run`. Full suite: 2751 passed, 28 skipped, 0 failed.

Not wired into a pre-commit hook or the bump workflow itself — it's a standalone script to run manually (`scripts/check_epoch_activation.py [--field X --artifact-glob Y] [--skip-vps]`) before/after committing a boundary, matching the "checklist/script" framing this task was registered under. Automatic invocation from the bump workflow, if wanted, is a future task.

See DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-21 (T-388 row), `scripts/check_epoch_activation.py`, `src/prediction_market_bot/logic_epoch.py`.

## T-391 — GG-128: `session_delta.sh` OPEN-gap parser undercounted (10 vs. true 18) — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-20

While running the mandatory `/session-start` protocol (Step 3, open governance gaps) and cross-checking the ADR-0041 delta-first path's output against `governance_scan.sh`'s authoritative grep, the two counts diverged: `scripts/session_delta.sh` Section 2b reported **10** OPEN gaps, `governance_scan.sh` reported **18**. Investigation of the script's embedded Python heredoc found three compounding bugs: (1) `.startswith("OPEN")` doesn't match bold-markdown-prefixed Status cells (`**OPEN — ...`) — the same defect GG-122 already fixed in `governance_scan.sh`'s grep, never ported to this sibling script; (2) the original entry-extraction did an unbounded forward blob-search for the next `| Status |` cell from each Gap ID match, which bled into the *next* entry's Status text whenever the current entry had none (GG-035/GG-036 use a terminal `| Resolution |` field instead); (3) even bounded per-entry, a blob-level regex search matched an inline quoted example of bad Status-line text embedded in GG-122's own "What was missed" prose (which quotes `"| Status | **OPEN — ..."` as part of describing this exact bug class) before reaching GG-122's real, later Status row — a "quine" false positive.

**Fix:** rewrote Section 2b to walk the file line-by-line (mirroring `governance_scan.sh`'s own `^\| Status \|` line-anchored grep) rather than blob-searching: track `current_gid` via a `### GG-\d+` header match, require `^\|\s*Status\s*\|` to match at true line-start before associating a cell with that gap ID (immune to bugs 2 and 3, since a table row must be its own physical line), and replace the `.startswith` check with `re.match(r"\*{0,2}OPEN\b", ...)` plus `governance_scan.sh`'s existing two exclusions (fixing bug 1). Verified: re-run against the live checkpoint now returns exactly `18 OPEN gap(s)`, matching `governance_scan.sh`'s list item-for-item. Sections 2a/2c/2d (bankroll, EXP-deltas, gate progress) untouched, still run clean.

**Impact:** every session that used the ADR-0041 delta-first default path as its session-start retrieval mechanism (rather than the full `governance_scan.sh` run) was silently shown an incomplete open-gap list, missing 8 real items including GG-046 (this session's own originating watch item) and GG-045/GG-124/GG-125.

See DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-20 (T-391 row), GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-128 (new, RESOLVED).

## T-382 — GG-105 ba_l10 empirical-Bayes shrinkage (APR-0025/ADR-0047): ✅ DONE, 2026-07-17

Earl signed off on APR-0025 in full and resolved three DS-reserved design parameters (K=40 shrinkage strength; real per-player `ab_sum` as the observed-sample-size term, replacing the flat `AB_WINDOW=37` placeholder; ship shrinkage alone, defer H2 pregame-blend to its own follow-on — see T-384 below). Implemented `ba_l10_shrunk = (ab_sum*ba_l10 + K*PRIOR)/(ab_sum + K)` in `mlb_features.py`; surfaced audit-only in `run_mlb_pregame.py` (T1's frozen C3 model does not consume it, per Hard Rule 4); wired into `run_t4_mlb_live.py`'s live Binomial calc, which actually drives T4M-PROJ. New regression test `test_ba_l10_shrunk_drives_p_t4_not_raw_ba_l10` added to `tests/test_run_t4_mlb_live.py`, proving the shrunk value (not raw `ba_l10`) drives the actual computed `p_t4` through the full `_run_cycle()` path. Full suite: **2730 passed, 29 skipped, 0 failed** (+9 vs. 2721 baseline). **GG-105 stays OPEN** — this ships the fix; it does not itself constitute the fresh post-fix clean-epoch tercile-monotonicity re-measurement required before GG-105 can be marked RESOLVED (blocked on `clean_n` accrual under the resulting epoch advance — confirm/track that advance next session). ## T-367 — Live AB_WINDOW re-pull: replace `run_t359_gg105_interaction_model.py`'s fixed `AB_WINDOW=37` with real per-player rolling AB sums, re-run H1 sweep — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-21 (Window B)

The only source is the live MLB Stats API (`fetch_batter_game_log`, `mlb_ingestion.py:357-411`), one call per unique player (75 unique players across the epoch-3 clean cohort, 666 rows), at the documented `DEFAULT_REQUEST_DELAY_S=0.6` rate-limit floor (~45s minimum serial pull).
- Scope: pull all 75 players' game logs, re-derive each row's real 10-game rolling AB sum as of its `game_date` (same `.shift(1).rolling(10, min_periods=3).sum()` logic already in `mlb_features.py`), re-run `run_t359_gg105_interaction_model.py`'s H1 shrinkage sweep with per-row AB counts instead of the fixed constant, diff the resulting Fisher-p/win-rate-gap table against the existing k=37-fixed baseline (`exp_output/T359-GG105-INTERACTION-MODEL/interaction_model_results.json`).
- Not blocking APR-0025 sign-off — see `05_RESEARCH_EXPERIMENTS/RESULTS_SUMMARIES/T366_APR0025_DECISION_BRIEF.md`. Best run as its own isolated-worktree session with live network access, not bundled into an unrelated task. Real 10-game rolling AB sum distribution across the 666 joined rows: mean=37.30, median=38.0, stdev=3.97, min=15, max=45 — centers almost exactly on the fixed constant. Re-ran H1 sweep with real per-row AB counts substituted for AB_WINDOW=37: every verdict at every k (0/20/40/80/160) reproduces the k=37-fixed baseline exactly (ANTI-PREDICTIVE at k=0/20/40/80, INCONCLUSIVE at k=160), with Fisher-p differences only in the third-plus decimal place. **Confirms, does not reverse, T-359's finding** — APR-0025's k=40 recommendation and its live deployment (ADR-0047, epoch 8, 2026-07-18) are unaffected; this was retrospective validation of the research sweep, not a check on production `ba_l10_shrunk` (which already uses real `ab_sum`, per ADR-0047). ## T-351 — GG-116 leaked-credential remediation: operator decision + execution on git-history scrub and Kalshi key rotation — DONE (2026-07-13)

**Why this is a task, not just a note:** GG-116's Resolution field explicitly recommended "registering as a fresh T-NNN when the operator decides on remediation scope" — that registration never happened when GG-116 (then GG-114, then GG-113) was first written, and had been left as prose only across `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md`/`DECISION_LOG.md`/`TODO_NEXT.md`'s narrative paragraphs. Caught and fixed during a 2026-07-12 close-out re-audit (operator explicitly asked whether all auxiliary findings from T-346/T-347 had been completed, not just the two named tasks).

**Scope:** (1) operator decides whether the leaked Kalshi API key ID + private-key path (redacted from the live file tree in commit `c7be13a`, still present in git history at `dce071e` and after) needs a git-history scrub (`git filter-repo` or equivalent — destructive, force-push, coordinate with any other active branches first) vs. is adequately mitigated by (2) rotating the actual Kalshi credential so the historical value is inert regardless of history-scrub decision. Rotation alone (without a history scrub) is the lower-risk, faster mitigation since it makes the leaked value harmless without a destructive git operation. See `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-116, `THREAT_MODEL.md` TH-02.

**2026-07-13 update:** Item (2) done — operator revoked the leaked key in the Kalshi demo dashboard and generated a replacement; Claude executed the mechanical cutover (new key installed local + VPS, `t4-mlb.service` restarted, new key verified authenticating live, `get_balance()` → $995.05). **Item (1), the history-scrub decision, is also now done.** Operator explicitly decided to proceed ("Yes - proceed with your recommendation. Keep the clean hygiene - no need to defer."), then, on learning 4 concurrent terminal sessions were active (this session, a GG-113 worktree, a hardware test-suite terminal, a crash-residue cleanup terminal), directed proceeding "with caution" to avoid stranding their work. Claude re-verified worktree/branch state (only `origin/main` needed a force-push; other sessions' branches were local-only and their uncommitted diffs are immune to history rewrite by construction), took a precautionary backup of the GG-113 worktree's uncommitted diff as zero-cost insurance, then executed `git filter-repo --replace-text` against a mirror clone, verified zero occurrences of the leaked key ID across all refs (5→0), and force-pushed `refs/heads/main` (`df50835` → `47096dd`). Propagated to the local main tree and VPS deploy clone (both now at `47096dd`; `t4-mlb.service` confirmed still active post-sync, no tracked-file drift). **Known residual, disclosed:** GitHub's 20 `refs/pull/N/head` PR refs are read-only and could not be force-pushed over, so old commits with the (now-revoked) key ID may still be viewable via GitHub's PR UI/API — a hygiene footnote on a dead credential, not an active exposure. GG-116 flipped to full RESOLVED. See `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-116 and `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-13 for full detail.

## T-353 — Evaluate systemd hardening parity for `t4-mlb.service` — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-22 (Window B)

**Why this is a task:** GG-093's Resolution field flagged this as a residual item ("recommend evaluating hardening parity... before G6") but never created one. `deploy/t4-mlb.service` (the unit that loads `/opt/prediction-market-bot/.env` via `EnvironmentFile=` and runs as `root`, holding the live Kalshi RSA credential) carried zero systemd hardening directives, while `scripts/pmbot-web-dashboard.service` (which needs no secrets at all) has `PrivateTmp`, `NoNewPrivileges`, `ReadOnlyPaths`, `MemoryMax`, and `CPUQuota`. Scope: evaluate whether `t4-mlb.service` can adopt the same (or equivalent) hardening directives without breaking its `.env`-loading or live-poll functionality, and implement if safe. See `THREAT_MODEL.md` TH-02.

**Done:** partial parity, not full — evaluated each directive against this service's actual behavior rather than copy-pasting the dashboard's set. **Adopted:** `PrivateTmp=true`/`NoNewPrivileges=true` (verified zero functional risk — grepped `run_t4_mlb_live.py`, confirmed it never touches `/tmp`, its singleton lock lives under `_LOGS_DIR`; the service never needs setuid/setgid); `MemoryMax=512M`/`CPUQuota=50%` (sized from live VPS telemetry pulled at deploy time — steady-state RSS 95-109M peak over a 2-day run, ~2.5% average CPU on the box's single core — leaving ~5x/~20x headroom, bounding a runaway loop without risking normal-operation OOM/throttle on a single-core, ~1GB-RAM VPS). **Deliberately not adopted:** the dashboard's `ReadOnlyPaths=logs/` directive — copying it verbatim would make `t4-mlb`'s own `logs/` writes (portfolio JSON, audit JSONL, singleton lock) fail on first write, since (unlike the read-only dashboard) this service writes there continuously; and `ProtectSystem=strict` — would need its own staged test against every path this process and its dependencies touch, out of scope for this pass, flagged as a candidate follow-up rather than force-implemented against the only live trading pipeline. Deployed live: confirmed no game was in-play (all `STATUS_SCHEDULED` as of 2026-07-22 05:57 UTC) before restarting; `systemctl restart t4-mlb` came back clean (`Memory: ... max: 512.0M` confirms the cap applied), normal ESPN-polling loop resumed identically within 5s. Full rationale documented inline in `deploy/t4-mlb.service`'s own comments.

## T-343 — PIPE-05 market-fetch retry/backoff for GG-110 (SemanticFloorViolation false-fire on slow Kalshi publish) — ✅ DONE, 2026-07-11 (implementation session, isolated worktree `session/20260711T231206Z-t343-pipe05-retry-backoff`)

**Implemented:** replaced PIPE-05's one-shot market-fetch+group step in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` (Step 5) with a bounded linear-backoff retry loop. New module-level constants (both env-overridable): `PIPE05_FLOOR_RETRY_ATTEMPTS = 3` (default), `PIPE05_FLOOR_RETRY_DELAY_SEC = 300` (default; per-attempt delay = `attempt_number * 300s`, i.e. 300s/600s/900s across the 3 attempts, ~30 min total worst case). Retry fires only when `_count_scheduled_mlb_games(GAME_DATE_ISO) > 0` (games are scheduled today) but the current fetch matched 0 players — the exact "Kalshi hasn't finished publishing yet" signature from GG-110. A genuine 0-scheduled-games day breaks immediately on the first pass, no retry burned. Each retry attempt logs a `PIPE-05`/`semantic_floor_retry` info-level C6 audit event before sleeping. Verified the max retry window (~30 min at defaults) cannot collide with PIPE-05.5 (T2, 19:30 UTC), which fires >8h after PIPE-05's 11:00 UTC start.

**Tests:** extended the existing GG-054 drill class (`TestMainSemanticFloorViolationFiresLive` in `tests/test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py`) with two new end-to-end tests plus retry-specific assertions on the pre-existing test: `test_retry_recovers_when_later_fetch_matches_players` (first fetch 0-matched, second fetch recovers — confirms exactly 1 retry, no violation, `main()` completes normally) and `test_no_retry_on_genuine_zero_games_day` (0 scheduled games — confirms 0 retries, 0 sleeps, immediate fall-through). All three tests mock `_build_live_sp_override`/`MLBIngestionClient` — discovered mid-session that the pre-existing SP-overlay call site (Step 3b, unrelated to T-343) was making a real, unmocked MLB Stats API call in every test in this class, which itself calls `time.sleep()` internally for rate-limiting; since `time.sleep` is a shared stdlib singleton, patching it for the retry-loop assertions also silently intercepted that unrelated call, inflating observed sleep counts by 1 and initially producing confusing `assert 4 == 3`/`assert 2 == 1` failures. Mocking the SP-overlay call directly (matching how other test classes in this file already isolate external calls) fixed this and is also a latent-bug fix in its own right — this test class had been making live network calls with no assertion depending on the result, a source of potential CI flakiness unrelated to T-343. Full suite: **2648 passed, 29 skipped, 0 failed** (up from 2465/29/0 at T-336/T-337 baseline — net +183 tests reflects work merged by other sessions in the interim plus this session's 2 new tests).

**Impact:** any future day where Kalshi is slow to publish same-day KXMLBHIT markets by PIPE-05's 11:00 UTC fire now gets up to 3 re-fetch attempts spread over ~30 minutes before SemanticFloorGuard declares a genuine violation — closing the silent 0-bets-placed failure mode GG-110 diagnosed. T4M-PROJ was and remains structurally unaffected (independent 45s-poll evaluator).

**Depends on:** none. GG-110 diagnosis (prior session). **Fully closed — code, tests, docs.**

## T-330/T-331 — Workstream D: ledger-driven MC forward projection + bootstrap CI (✅ MERGED TO MAIN, 2026-07-11, `81cfb20`)

**Both tasks complete.** `scripts/mc_forward_projection.py` gained `--from-ledger {T1,T2,T3,T4M-PROJ}` (T-330/D1): `derive_ledger_stats()` reads win_rate/win_mult/loss_mult/stake_frac/cadence directly from `filter_clean()`-ed live ledger records (reusing `run_band_monitor.py`'s loaders and clean_n filter verbatim, `run_power_calc.py`'s cadence derivation) instead of the module's hardcoded constants, and emits three explicit scenarios per band: **floor** (zero-edge breakeven win_rate — "what if the edge is illusory"), **operational** (the frozen `SimParams()` defaults — the original GG-071/ADR-0040 regression anchor, untouched), **ceiling** (fully observed win_rate/mult/stake from the ledger). `--bootstrap` (T-331/D2) resamples clean records with replacement (`n_boot=30, boot_sims=500` defaults — tuned down from an initial 200/1000 after empirical timing showed `simulate_paths()` cost is dominated by `days*bets_per_day` loop iterations, not `n_sims`; high-cadence T4M-PROJ costs ~1s/resample regardless) and reports P(kill) as a median + 90% CI band per horizon, e.g. "P(kill 1yr) = X% [90% CI: lo%–hi%]", directly on `build_ledger_report()`'s floor/ceiling scenarios (operational is unaffected by ledger sampling by design, so no CI is computed for it).

**Live smoke test results (not asserted in tests, informational only):** T1 (clean_n=65) floor shows 69.6%/100%/100% kill risk (30d/182d/365d) vs. ceiling (real edge) 2.8%/17.7%/17.8% — stark contrast confirms the scenario framework correctly isolates edge-vs-mechanics. T4M-PROJ (clean_n=271) ceiling shows ~0% kill risk at all horizons (win_rate=0.6568 relative to very low stake_frac≈0.057%); floor still shows material 1yr risk (~7.25%). T1's ceiling bootstrap CI is very wide (1yr: 0%–100% at n_boot=30) — expected, honest reflection of small-sample (clean_n=65) uncertainty, not a bug. T2 (clean_n=0) correctly raises a clean `ValueError`/`SystemExit(1)` rather than crashing.

**Tests:** `tests/test_mc_forward_projection.py` grew from 11 to 33 tests (all new classes: `TestDeriveLedgerStats`, `TestBuildLedgerScenarios`, `TestLoadCleanBandRecords`, `TestBootstrapKillCI`, `TestBuildLedgerReport`), synthetic/hermetic (`_mk_recs()` helper, no live-file dependency), all passing. Full repo suite run: 2464 passed, 29 skipped, **1 pre-existing failure** (`test_band_monitor.py::TestT1RegressionAnchor` — already logged as GG-106, live-ledger-drift pinning issue, unrelated to this workstream, not touched by this branch). Exit criteria for both D1 ("one command reproduces the A/B/C/E-style table from live data, no hand-entered constants") and D2 ("MC output reads as a CI band, not a single number") are met.

## T-329 — GG-105 DS-review diagnostic (1) of 3: size-weighted tercile re-run — DONE (2026-07-10)

**Did:** Selected and ran diagnostic (1) of the three GG-105 proposed diagnostics (`T324_T1_VS_T4_DIVERGENCE_MEMO.md`) first: size-weighted re-run of T4M-PROJ's clean_n edge-tercile inversion, testing whether Kelly sizing compounds the win-rate inversion's P&L damage. Built `scripts/run_t329_size_weighted_diag.py` (reuses `run_band_monitor.py`'s `load_t4_ledger`/`filter_clean` and `ab_harness.metrics()` verbatim). **Result: sizing does NOT amplify the inversion — it's inverted.** Avg stake falls across edge terciles (does not rise), and $-weighted return (`pnl_per_dollar_staked`) rises across terciles (does not fall) — tercile 3 (lowest win rate, 58.2%) has the best $/staked return of the three. Overall clean_n cohort: +8.12% pnl/staked. Candidate (b) (sizing-as-amplifier) is ruled out as GG-105 characterized it. Full detail: DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-10 (T-329 row), GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-105.

## T-332 — GG-105 DS-review diagnostic (2) of 3: game-state segmentation — DONE (2026-07-11)

**Did:** Registered T-332 (operator: "register and then proceed with addressing with full depth") and ran diagnostic (2): game-state segmentation of T4M-PROJ's clean_n edge-tercile inversion, testing candidate (a) (real signal / game-state noise — T4's core latency thesis). Discovered and empirically validated (271/271, 100%, 0 collisions) a join from resolved position → live-poll cycle via `forecast_id`'s embedded `cycle_id`, against `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl`'s `(cycle_id, ticker)` key — two other candidate join sources (`paper_portfolio_mlb_t4.json` closed positions, `audit_mlb_t4_edge_decisions.jsonl`) were checked and ruled out first, neither carries game-state fields. Built `scripts/run_t332_gamestate_diag.py` (reuses `run_band_monitor.py` loaders + the audited `run_exp017_mlb.edge_monotonicity()` Fisher test unmodified). **Result: the inversion is NOT concentrated in one game-state slice.** Both buckets reaching `MIN_N=30` verdict ANTI-PREDICTIVE independently: `inning=early_1-3` (n=136) and `ab_remaining=2+` (n=213, majority of cohort). `mid_4-6` (n=133) and `ab_remaining=1` (n=58) are directionally the same but INCONCLUSIVE at the Fisher bar; `late_7+` (n=2) UNDERPOWERED. Zero buckets verdicted MONOTONIC. Game-state correlations with edge/win were all weak (|r|≤0.13). **Two new data-quality gaps found and logged:** `t4_mlb_cycle_log.jsonl` has no score-differential field for T4M-PROJ either (mirrors the already-documented T4M-FLOW gap); `batting_slot` is hardcoded/near-constant `0` in 99.6% of all cycle-log rows, not a usable signal. GG-105 Resolution/Status updated in place. **Both candidate (a) game-state-noise and candidate (b) sizing-artifact are now weighed against** — the edge computation itself is the leading unexplained factor remaining. Full detail: DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-11 (T-332 row), GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-105.
**Next recommended:** Diagnostic (3) — re-run as clean_n grows past its immature-cadence window (<14 days) — is the last of the three originally-scoped diagnostics and remains unstarted, but is a passive re-check rather than a build. Higher-value next step: a targeted look at T4M-PROJ's `p_t4`/edge/fee-net-edge formula itself (or an upstream feature feeding it), since both game-state and sizing have now been ruled against as external confounds and the anti-predictive rank-ordering has to originate somewhere.

Confirmed exact threshold by direct probe: `temperature_scale(1.0, T)` saturates to exactly `1.0` for `T≤~0.55`; `T=0.6` is the largest still-unsaturated sub-1.0 temperature (`0.9999999999999989`). Confirmed asymmetric — the `p→0` side does not saturate the same way (underflow-to-zero is a far more extreme threshold than addition-rounding). Locked in as a dedicated test (`test_sharpening_temperature_can_saturate_extreme_p_to_exact_boundary`) with exact reproduction values rather than hidden by narrowing the Hypothesis strategy — same "document, don't hide" discipline as B2's ECE finding. B2: built `tests/test_t280_cross_implementation_invariants.py` (83 tests) — Brier (`monitor.py::_compute_brier` vs `run_exp017c_calibration_mlb.py::brier_murphy`), temperature scaling (`calibration.py::temperature_scale` vs a hand-derived algebraic power-mean closed form), and ECE (`monitor.py::_compute_ece` vs the script's `ece()`) all cross-checked; Kelly's cross-check was already covered in B1, not duplicated. **Found a real, narrow bug via this check, not a false alarm:** `monitor.py` builds ECE bin edges via `np.linspace(0,1,11)` while the script uses pure division `i/10` — 3 of 10 interior edges are different floats (confirmed: `np.linspace(0,1,11)[3]==0.30000000000000004 != 3/10==0.3`, same at indices 6,7), so a `p_estimate` landing exactly on one of those 3 points is binned differently by each implementation. Documented in a dedicated test (`test_exact_decile_inputs_can_legitimately_diverge_between_implementations`) with root cause, exact drifted indices, and magnitude locked in, rather than silently avoided by picking different test data. Assessed as negligible-severity (real `p_estimate` values are continuous statistical outputs, essentially never exact decimal literals — measure-zero collision risk) and NOT fixed in production code this session (out of scope for a test-only task; would require choosing one bin-edge convention as canonical in live C7 monitoring code). **T-281 registered** (new, low-priority, unclaimed as a separate SESSION_CLAIMS row since it's a direct sub-finding of the already-claimed T-280 window): unify `monitor.py`'s and `run_exp017c_calibration_mlb.py`'s ECE bin-edge construction onto one convention (prefer pure division — matches the script's already-correct-looking `i/bins` and avoids `np.linspace`'s multiplication drift) — not urgent, do opportunistically. Full suite after B2: 2504 passed, 30 skipped, 0 failed. DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-09 entry has full derivation. B3 (Hypothesis property tests), B4 (ECE/reliability/Murphy lift into a shared module), B5 (golden tests + seeded conftest), B6 (final close) remain unstarted.

> **Thread 7 — GG-105 escalated from diagnosis to a formal remediation proposal (2026-07-15, session run only after independently re-verifying, not trusting, Window 1's GG-113 deploy).** T-359 built the first GG-105 test that manipulates the suspected mechanism rather than measuring around it: empirical-Bayes shrinkage of `ba_l10` swept across k∈{0,20,40,80,160} against the frozen epoch-3 clean_n cohort (n=666, hardcoded pre-GG-113-epoch-reset). Found a clean, monotonic dose-response — Fisher p rises <0.0001→0.0045→0.0797→0.1552 as shrinkage strengthens, the anti-predictive win-rate gap compressing from 0.530/0.716 to 0.563/0.619 — direct support for H1 (unshrunk `ba_l10` treated as a fixed Bernoulli parameter) as a real contributing mechanism. H4 (`ab_remaining` compounding that noise) could not be independently isolated (only 12/666, 1.8%, have real known-`batting_slot` data, below `MIN_N=20`). **T-361 converted this into `00_ADMIN/APR-0025_gg105-ba_l10-shrinkage-remediation.md`, Status PROPOSED, awaiting DS sign-off** — proposes shrinking `ba_l10` toward the cohort's own observed-mean prior (0.2512) at `p_hits_gte_threshold()`'s consumption point, explicitly leaves shrinkage strength `k` as a DS decision (naming k=40 only as the lightest-touch value already reaching significance, not a recommendation), flags `AB_WINDOW=37` as an unverified heuristic that should be replaced with real per-player `ab_sum` in any implementation, and states plainly — not glossed over — that the anti-predictive pattern's *direction* persists even at k=160: this is proposed as partial mitigation only, with H2 (blending `p_t1` into `p_t4`, structurally discarded today) named as necessary separate follow-on not yet tested. Per Hard Rule 3/ADR-0039 Tier B precedent, this APR does not itself authorize implementation — it requests sign-off to draft a superseding ADR. **GG-105 remains OPEN.** No live code touched this session; `run_t4_mlb_live.py`'s formula is unchanged pending DS review. Full detail: `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 (T-361 row), `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-105 (OPEN, T-361 addendum), `00_ADMIN/APR-0025_gg105-ba_l10-shrinkage-remediation.md`.
>
> **Thread 8 — GG-119 (T1 MLB vig-sign-flip parallel to GG-113) CONFIRMED and quantified, T2 RULED OUT (2026-07-15, T-362, isolated worktree `session/20260715T054123Z-gg119-t2-vig-diagnosis`, diagnosis-only per explicit instruction).** Read `run_mlb_paper_trade.py::_directional_edge()` and T2's local gating formula directly against `run_exp017.py`'s symmetric-vig convention, the same bar GG-113 was tested against. **T1 carries the identical bug:** `directional_gross = gross_edge if direction=="YES" else -gross_edge` negates a `gross_edge` that already has `half_spread` subtracted as a cost, flipping that cost into a credit for every NO bet — algebraically identical to GG-113's pattern in `run_t4_mlb_live.py`, ADR-0043. **T2 RULED OUT** — `run_t2_mlb_sameday.py` has no NO-direction branch at all, so the negation step cannot execute for T2. Quantified empirically against the live 259-position resolved T1 ledger (`scripts/run_t362_gg119_t1_vig_diag.py`, mirroring `run_t344_vig_sign_diag.py`'s method): all 248 NO bets inflated by exactly `2 × half_spread`; **74 of those (28.57% of the entire resolved T1 book) only cleared `MIN_EDGE_TO_APPROVE=0.04` (`risk.py` CHECK 3) because of the inflation, and that 74-bet subgroup is net-losing at -$48.26 aggregate P&L against a book-wide total of +$34.56** — meaning the bug is currently *helping* T1's reported P&L look worse than a corrected formula would, not better (removing the falsely-admitted drag would improve book P&L by ≈$82.82). No GG-105-style monotonic anti-predictive signature found under the corrected framing — this stands as an independent, dollar-denominated G6-gate-integrity finding on the active/primary book, not a repeat of GG-105. Adjacent, explicitly out-of-scope: T2 uses full `vig` rather than `half_spread` as its cost term — same class of open question as ADR-0043's still-open T4M-PROJ-scoped magnitude note, not resolved here. **No code changed in either production file**, per the explicit instruction that the bar for touching T1's live formula is higher than it was for GG-113/T4M-PROJ (T1/T2 is the active/primary G6 book). GG-119's Status remains **OPEN pending DS sign-off** (Hard Rule 3). Packaged as a decision request for Earl in **PR #22** (https://github.com/eignacio1/prediction-market-bot/pull/22), same escalation shape as GG-113/ADR-0043 — awaiting authorization before any fix, epoch bump, or T2-magnitude follow-up. Full detail: `DECISION_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 (T-362 row), `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md` GG-119 (OPEN, confirmed/quantified), `TODO_NEXT.md` T-362, `exp_output/T362-GG119-T1-VIG-DIAG/t1_vig_sign_diag_results.json`.

> **S118 (CONTINUATION #3) DELTA — GG-081/T-236 root-caused and fixed; sibling defect GG-083/T-238 discovered and fixed in the same commit.** Root cause: `run_t4_mlb_live.py`'s date-rollover block reassigns `game_date` to the new calendar day at UTC midnight, then reconstructs both `already_fired_game` (T4 per-ticker re-entry dedup, GG-076/T-225) and the T4 band budget ledger, filtering on an exact string match against the new date. A game still live across UTC midnight keeps its positions dated the prior day, so both reconstructions silently lost all memory of that game the instant the clock rolled over — explaining the 2/8 "identical-state" re-entries found in item 4 (the dedup guard had actually forgotten the ticker's prior state, not detected a real state change) and, distinctly, GG-083: the T4 band budget ledger has the identical exact-date-match defect, meaning budget consumed by a carried-over game silently vanishes at midnight, understating deployed T4 capital. Confirmed via ground truth: the anomalous tickers (`TBJCAMINERO13-1`/`-2`) shared one `session_id` across both fires, proving a live logic defect, not a restart artifact (T-225's re-entry redesign itself is NOT implicated — this is a reconstruction-scope bug in the surrounding rollover code, not the dedup key design). **Fix:** new `_game_date_window(game_date: str) -> set` helper widens both reconstructions to `{game_date, prior_day}`; `_reconstruct_t4_ledger` and `_reconstruct_fired_ticker_states` now accept `Union[str, set]`, fully backward-compatible with all 12 pre-existing unit tests that pass a bare string. Safety argument: Kalshi tickers embed the real game date and matchup, so widening the reconstruction window cannot cross-contaminate two distinct games sharing a ticker prefix. **6 new tests, full suite 2339 passed / 7 skipped / 1 pre-existing unrelated failure (`test_feature_csv_freshness`, environmental) — zero regressions, up from 2293.** Also corrected a stale CLAUDE.md Hard Rule 9 arithmetic inconsistency ("4.6 and 3.7 bets/game" → "5.75 and 3.67", matching the T-235/GG-080 source data already correct elsewhere in the same file). T-225 was explicitly flagged in its own task description as "not a call for a model to make unilaterally" — surfaced to Earl via AskUserQuestion. **Earl's ruling: "allow re-entry per ticker."** Full rationale (preserved verbatim in DECISION_LOG.md): the permanent one-shot dedup was correct scaffolding for an unproven pipeline, but the pipeline is now proven (kill switch drilled at 0.34ms, contamination guard verified, edge math confirmed responsive to real Kalshi price moves); real backstops now exist (`max_single_event_exposure_usd`, drawdown kill switch, T4 band budget) that should carry over-concentration risk instead of the blunt dedup proxy; the T4 alpha thesis is specifically about a *repeated* phenomenon (market latency), and a permanent one-shot cap structurally prevents gathering enough evidence to prove or disprove it; CLAUDE.md Hard Rule 9's ~1,000-eval/game throughput target is "back-derived from how much evidence the thesis needs to be provable," not arbitrary, and matching it is the real target; T-226 (venue coverage, ~6-7/9 games/day) was already resolved favorably, satisfying his one caveat. Closing instruction: "We're proceeding now - apply accordingly." **Implementation:** `already_fired_game` in `run_t4_mlb_live.py` changed from `set[str]` to `dict[str, tuple[int, int]]` keyed on `(h_so_far, ab_remaining)` — the exact two live-state scalars driving `p_t4`'s Binomial computation — so a ticker may re-fire once that state has actually moved, and a same-state re-check remains a blocked no-op re-fire. `PaperTrader.open_position()` gained two new optional kwargs (`h_so_far_at_entry`, `ab_remaining_at_entry`; default `None`, backward-compatible for non-T4 callers) to persist the state snapshot on each position record. `_reconstruct_fired_ticker_states()` (renamed from `_reconstruct_fired_tickers`) rebuilds the guard from the persisted portfolio on process restart, iterating `open_positions + closed_positions` in `entered_at` order so the most recent entry per ticker wins regardless of open/closed status — correctly handling the case where an earlier fire on a ticker has since closed but a later re-entry is still open. Verified architecturally safe before writing code: neither `_resolve_pool()` (`run_resolve_mlb.py`) nor `PaperTrader.settle_position()` assume ticker uniqueness — both key on `position_id` (UUID), so multiple concurrent/sequential positions on the same Kalshi ticker resolve and settle independently with no conflict. **Tests:** 11 new (6 for `_reconstruct_fired_ticker_states` covering empty/mapped/pool-isolation-exclusion/date-exclusion/missing-fields-exclusion/most-recent-wins-across-open-and-closed; 3 for `_run_cycle`'s re-entry-blocked-when-unchanged / re-entry-allowed-when-changed / fired-position-records-entry-state; 2 for `PaperTrader.open_position()`'s new fields present-when-provided / defaults-none-for-non-T4-callers). Investigated via a background Explore agent; confirmed by direct code read: **`fetch_actual_hits`/`_fetch_mlb_hits` (`run_resolve_mlb.py:77-138`) has no game-completion gate at all** — it queries the MLB Stats API `gameLog` endpoint, which updates live, and returns whatever hit total is currently posted for the matching date regardless of game status. Production's `_resolve_pool()` incidentally avoids exposure via a `game_date < today` calendar filter (not a true completion check — doubleheaders/suspended/late-finishing games all evade it); the new backtest runners call `fetch_actual_hits` directly with no such filter, exactly reproducing the failure. **Registered GG-069** (OPEN, MEDIUM — correctness defect in a resolution primitive shared with production paper-trade resolution; zero capital risk today, since no live capital exists and the corrupted resolutions were in a research backtest, not a real bet). **This means the EXP-ARITH-MLB-01 real-run result must not be read as simply "insufficient data" — the handful of records that did resolve are known-invalid, not just small-sample; no directional signal (positive or negative) should be drawn from this run under any grid cell until GG-069 is fixed and a new snapshot is frozen against independently-confirmed-FINAL games.** EXP-FLOW-MLB-01 was not attempted against this snapshot — `reconstruct_mlb_score_diff.py` correctly requires ESPN `STATUS_FINAL` and fails closed on today's in-progress games. > **S101 DELTA:** **Principal vision, alpha taxonomy, and market efficiency gradient formalized as canonical CLAUDE.md content.** Three structural sections added to CLAUDE.md that every future session now reads before any work: (1) `## Principal Vision` — "This is a calibration engine for probabilistic reasoning, structured as a trading bot." Prediction markets chosen because they provide all three simultaneously: binary-resolved outcomes, short feedback loops, market-implied probability as explicit benchmark. Canonical corollary: "P&L is the score. The methodology is the asset." (2) `### Alpha taxonomy` — T1 alpha (pregame calibration edge via historical rolling stats) and T4 alpha (live market latency edge via 45-90s polling) are independent hypotheses requiring separate commercial validation. A T1 verdict does not transfer to T4 and vice versa. EXP-017 is the T1 track. GG-046 is the T4 track. The ~1,000 evaluations/game is a structural consequence of the T4 thesis, not a mandate. (3) `### Market efficiency gradient` — MLB mid-week regular season ($500-$2,000 book depth, low public attention, slower price discovery) is the target environment. Maximally liquid markets (NBA Finals) are hostile conditions — the GG-037 anti-predictive finding was produced there. Binding operational constraint: when adding a new market, the first question is not "do we have data?" but "is this market less efficient than a calibrated model can produce?" Secondary finding: Kalshi KXMLBHIT depth sets a capital ceiling structural to the venue; GG-045 registered (not addressable by improved modeling). This section now holds **three** concatenated chains, newest-first, from two different source documents, archived in two passes:
1. **2026-07-24 (session-end protocol tiering fix, Fix 1):** `TODO_NEXT.md`'s own "Last reviewed" chain (56 entries, 2026-07-17 through 2026-07-23/24), archived when that file was found to have regressed into the same inline-accumulation anti-pattern this section was created to fix.
2. **2026-07-24 (same pass, second cutover):** `MEMORY_BANK.md`'s own Project Identity chain had re-accumulated *again* since the original 2026-07-11 consolidation below — 53 entries, 2026-07-11 (T-397 close-out) through 2026-07-09 (Window B T-280 B1) — archived here immediately above the original chain, and `MEMORY_BANK.md` again replaced with a single consolidated `**Last reviewed:**` line.

All three chains are archived verbatim, newest-first within each chain, matching each live file's own ordering at time of archive. Entries from different source documents describing the same calendar dates (e.g. both files independently narrating 2026-07-09 through 2026-07-11 activity) are NOT duplicates — `MEMORY_BANK.md` and `TODO_NEXT.md` have always kept separate, independent "Last reviewed" narratives for the same underlying sessions; each is preserved as its own document's historical record. `check_memory_bank_snapshots.sh` extended in the 2026-07-11 pass to also count `**Last reviewed` header lines; still does not yet distinguish which source file re-accumulated (see Fix 1's guard-hardening step).

B2: swept `scripts/mc_survivable_edge_sweep.py` (new, wraps `mc_empirical_bootstrap.py`) for (a) minimum per-bet edge to hold 1yr P(kill)≤10% at today's real sizing/cadence — **MAIN +0.169%/bet** (~27x its best-ever observed cohort mean of +0.006%/bet); **T4 ≈ −0.0005%/bet** (already near-survivable at zero edge — T4 is edge-limited, not ruin-limited) — and (b) the Kelly-fraction/cadence/DD-width frontier by assumed edge. Key structural finding: Kelly-fraction cuts reduce variance **quadratically** (stake scaling), cadence cuts only **linearly** — at zero edge, cutting MAIN's Kelly to 10% survives (P(kill)=1.2%) but cutting cadence to 10% does not (P(kill)=79%), nor does widening the DD switch to 50% (P(kill)=71.5%). **Implication for a future Window B3 ADR: if MAIN's real edge doesn't clear ~+0.17%/bet, Kelly-fraction reduction is the only lever with enough leverage — cadence throttling and DD-width widening are both order-of-magnitude too weak alone.** Hit and resolved a live GG-ID collision mid-session-end (rebase conflicts in `GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md`/`DECISION_LOG.md`, both correctly non-`merge=union`) via the established renumber-the-unmerged-side-with-provenance-note convention (GG-113/GG-129→130/GG-131→132 precedent) — no destructive action, other session's entry untouched. Built `scripts/mc_survivable_edge_sweep.py` and swept the frontier: **MAIN needs +0.169%/bet edge to hold 1yr P(kill)≤10% at today's real sizing** (~27x its best-ever observed cohort mean of +0.006%/bet); **T4 needs only ≈−0.0005%/bet** (already near-survivable at zero edge — T4 is edge-limited, not ruin-limited, unlike MAIN). Structural finding for the eventual Window B3 ADR (superseding ADR-0040): Kelly-fraction cuts reduce variance quadratically while cadence cuts only reduce it linearly, so if MAIN's real edge can't clear ~+0.17%/bet, Kelly-fraction reduction is the only lever with enough leverage — cadence throttling and drawdown-width widening are both far too weak alone. **Last reviewed (prior):** 2026-07-13 (C8 dashboard OOM-kill fix, shared `main` tree) — Fixed `pmbot-web-dashboard.service`'s OOM-kill restart loop (**GG-118**), which had left the dashboard fully dark for ~2 days (operator-reported: "I'm blind!!"). Root cause: `scripts/web_dashboard.py`'s `_load_jsonl()` parsed an entire audit file into memory before slicing to the last `tail` entries — a scaling defect, not a `tail`-bounded one — which exceeded the service's `MemoryMax=128M` systemd cap once `audit_mlb.jsonl` (14MB)/`audit_mlb_t4.jsonl` (30MB) grew from two days of live MLB T1/T4 activity, triggering a `Restart=always`/`RestartSec=5` crash-loop invisible to plain `systemctl is-active` checks. Fixed via bounded `collections.deque(maxlen=tail)` buffering; verified with a synthetic 200k-line file (~15MB peak RSS), deployed to VPS, confirmed `MemoryCurrent`~20MB/`NRestarts=0` and end-to-end through the real SSH-tunnel path (`Open Dashboard.command`). No trading/capital impact — read-only monitoring gap, T1/T4 pipelines kept running normally throughout. Registered **T-356** (add a `pmbot-web-dashboard` health/`oom-kill` probe to CLAUDE.md's Step 4 — currently has zero coverage for this service). See DECISION_LOG.md 2026-07-13, GOVERNANCE_GAP_LOG.md GG-118, TODO_NEXT.md T-356.

Live end-to-end run against the real ledger shows flat sizing carries structurally lower forward ruin risk than T1's proportional regime (14.3% vs. ~46% at 1yr, floor scenario) since fixed-dollar stakes shrink as bankroll grows, unlike Kelly's constant-percentage compounding — a legitimate structural finding, not to be read in isolation from GG-105's edge-quality caveat. **Closing items done this continuation:** 17 new tests added (`tests/test_mc_forward_projection.py`, 33→50 passing) covering stake_mode validation, flat-mode simulation mechanics, frozen operational params, ledger-scenario/bootstrap-CI flat paths, and report-text assertions; a band-conditional GG-105 caveat block added inside `build_ledger_report()` (renders only for `band == "T4M-PROJ"`, states the ruin-risk figures describe sizing-mechanic behavior only and do not certify the edge is real — directs reader to GG-105). **Unplanned, fixed inline:** 2 pre-existing, unrelated `tests/test_cron_log_aggregator.py` failures found via full-suite pre-merge health check — a bare-substring date assertion (`OLD not in out`) coincidentally collided with an incidental GG-080 date citation in the aggregator's own output text; confirmed pre-existing via stash/pop, fixed by checking the per-day row-prefix format (`f"{OLD}:"`) instead — superseded during this branch's pre-merge rebase by an equivalent, already-merged upstream fix for the same flake, kept upstream's version. The general module-level-guard hazard in `run_t4_mlb_live.py` itself was deliberately left unfixed (touches the live, systemd-managed `t4-mlb.service` entry point; judged out of scope for a same-session bug fix — logged as a Decision Log judgment call).
